kaṭavuḷ ālattut taṭavuc ciṉaip pal paḻam - teyva muṟaiyum ālamarattiṉatu  periya kompiṉkaṭ pala paḻattai; nerunal uṇṭaṉam eṉṉātu - neṉṉalumuṇṭē meṉṉā vāy; piṉṉum celavu āṉā kali koḷ puḷḷiṉam - piṉṉum avviṭattup pōtalai yamaiyā oli poruntiṉa puḷḷiṉam; aṉaiyar iravalar - attaṉmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyār irappōr; avaraip puravetir koḷḷum peruñ cey yāṭavar uṭaimai yākum - avaraik kāttaṟku etirntukoḷḷum mikka ceykaiyaiyuṭaiya āṇ makkaḷatu celvamākum; avar uṭaimai - avvirappōr celvam; avar iṉmai yākum avar iṉmai - av vāṇmakkaḷuṭaiya vaṟumaiyākum avvirappōruṭaiya vaṟumai e-ṟu.  
vāḻiyum ōvum acainilai.
viḷakkam : 
ālamarattiṟ kaṭavuḷ uṟaiyu meṉpatu vaḻakku. “tuṟaiyu mālamum tolvaḻi marāmum, muṟaiyuḷip parāy” (kali. 104) eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. kaṭavuḷ ālattiṉ taṭavucciṉai pūttuk kāyttup paḻuttal kaṭaṉ; atu paḻamuṭaittātalait tērntu pōntuṇṭal, puḷḷiṉam appaḻaṅkaḷaic ceytukoḷḷa māṭṭāmaiyāṉ iṟaivaṉ paṭaitta amaippu. paḻaṅkaḷai yuṇṭu cellum puḷḷiṉam, vitaikaḷaip palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷiṟ corintu paḻumarattiṉ iṉattaip palkuvippatu pōla, iravalarum tamakkup paricil tantu ciṟappippār pukaḻai nāṭṭiṉ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷilum parappi, āṅkāṅku vaḷḷaṉmai yuṭaiyārait tōṟṟuvippar; ac ceyal vāyilāka valiyum paṭaiyu muṭaiyārpāl tokum poruḷ avaiyillārkkup payaṉpaṭṭu ulaka vāḻvaic cemmaippaṭuttum eṉpatu karuttu, itaṉāṟṟāṉ. “kalikoḷ puḷḷiṉam aṉaiyar iravalar” eṉṟār. mēlum, vaḷḷiyōraic cūḻntirukkum pulavar mutaliya iravalar, avar vaḻaṅkutaṟ keṭāvāṟu ceyvaṉa ceytaṟ kuriya uṟuti kūṟutalum avar ceyalātalpaṟṟi, “aṉaiya” reṉ ṟoḻiyātu, “peruñcey yāṭava ruṭaimaiyākumavaruṭaimai, avariṉmai yāku mavariṉ maiyē” eṉṟār. vaḷḷiyōrkkup poruḷuṟuti kāṭṭum nalattaic cāṉṟōrkaḷākiya kuṭapulaviyaṉār, uṟaiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār mutaliyōrālum, avariṉmai tamatākak karutip pēṇum nalattaik kapilar, picirāntaiyār, ēṇiccēri muṭamōciyār mutaliya cāṉṟōrkaḷālum aṟika. iravalarai vāḻttuvatu īṇṭuk karuttaṉmaiyiṉ, “vāḻiyō” eṉṟa viṭattu vāḻiyum ōvum acainilai yāyiṉa. 
200. viccikkō
viccikkō eṉpāṉ, iḷaṅkaṇṭīrakkōvōṭu naṭpuṟṟu avaṉōṭiruntu, peruntalaic cāttaṉārāl teruṭṭappaṭṭa iḷa viccikkōvukku muṉṉōṉ. vicci nāṭeṉṟum in nāṭṭavar vicciyareṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟupa. “vilkeḻu tāṉai vicciyar perumakaṉ” (kuṟun.328) eṉṟu āciriyar paraṇar kuṟippatu kāṇka. 
pāri yiṟantapiṉ, avaṉmakaḷiraik koṇarntu tiruk kōvalūril pārppāriṭattē aṭaikkalappaṭuttiya kapilar, avarkaḷait takka araca kumararkaṭku maṇampurivittal karuti, inta viccikkōvai yaṭaintār. viccikkōvum pāri makaḷirai maṇantukōṭaṟkuriya takutiyuṭaiyaṉā yiruntāṉ. viccik kōvum kapilarai varavēṟṟuc ciṟappittāṉ. avar avaṉai nōkki, “viccikkōvē! yāṉ koṇarntirukkum im makaḷir, mullaikku neṭuntēr aḷitta parantōṅkiya ciṟap piṉaiyuṭaiya vēḷ pāriyiṉ makaḷir. yāṉoru paricilaṉ; antaṇaṉ; yāṉ makaṭkoṭai puritaṟkut takutiyuṭaiyēṉ. ivarai maṇantu kōṭalē yāṉ niṉpāl peṟum paricil” eṉṟa karuttuppaṭum ippāṭṭaip pāṭiṉār. 
	paṉivarai nivanta pācilaip palaviṉ 
kaṉikavarn tuṇṭa karuviraṟ kaṭuvaṉ 
cemmuka mantiyoṭu ciṟantucēṇ viḷaṅki 
maḻaimicai yaṟiyā mālvarai yaṭukkattuk 
5	kaḻaimicait tuñcuṅ kallaka veṟpa
niṇantiṉṟu cerukkiya nerupputtalai neṭuvēṟ
kaḷaṅkoṇṭu kaṉaluṅ kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉai 
viḷaṅkumaṇik koṭumpūṇ viccik kōvē
ivarē, pūttalai yaṟāap puṉaikoṭi mullai
10 	nāttaḻum piruppap pāṭā tāyiṉum
kaṟaṅkumaṇi neṭuntēr koḷkeṉak koṭutta 
parantōṅku ciṟappiṟ pāri makaḷir 
yāṉē, paricilaṉ maṉṉu mantaṇa ṉīyē
varicaiyil vaṇakkum vāṇmēm paṭunaṉ 
15	niṉakkiyāṉ koṭuppak koṇmati ciṉappōr
aṭaṅkā maṉṉarai yaṭakkum
maṭaṅkā viḷaiyu ṇāṭukiḻa vōyē.	(200)
tiṇai : pāṭāṇṭiṉai; tuṟai : pariciṟṟuṟai. pārimakaḷirai viccikkōṉuḻaikkoṇṭuceṉṟa kapilar pāṭiyatu. 
urai : 
paṉi varai nivanta pācilaip palaviṉ kaṉi - kuḷirnta malaiyiṉkaṇ ōṅkiya paciya ilaiyaiyuṭaiya palāviṉatu paḻattai; kavarn tuṇṭa karuviral kaṭuvaṉ - kavarntuṇṭa kariya viralaiyuṭaiya kaṭuvaṉ; cemmuka mantiyoṭu ciṟantu - civanta mukattaiyuṭaiya taṉatu mantiyuṭaṉē polintu; cēṇ viḷaṅki - cēymaikkaṇṇē viḷaṅki; maḻai micai yaṟiyā mālvarai yaṭukkattu - mukilālum ucci yaṟiyappaṭāta uyarnta malaip pakkattu; kaḻaimicait tuñcum kallaka veṟpa - mūṅkilucciyiṉkaṇ tuyilum malaiyakattut tāḻnta varaiyai yuṭaiyōy; niṇam tiṉṟu cerukkiya nerupputtalai neṭu vēl - niṇattait tiṉṟu kaḷittē neruppuppōlum talaiyaiyuṭaiya neṭiya vēliṉaiyum; kaḷam koṇṭu kaṉalum kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉai - kaḷattait taṉatākkikkoṇṭu kāyum taṟukaṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉaiyum; viḷaṅku maṇik koṭumpūṇ viccikkōvē - viḷaṅkiya maṇikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷainta āparaṇattiṉaiyumuṭaiya viccik kōvē; ivar - ivarkaḷtām; pūttalai aṟāap puṉai koṭimullai - pūvait taṉatu talaiyiṉkaṇ māṟāta alaṅkarittāṟpōlum koṭi mullaitāṉ; nāt taḻumpu iruppap pāṭā tāyiṉum - nāt taḻumpēṟap pāṭātāyiṉum; kaṟaṅku maṇi neṭuntēr koḷka eṉak koṭutta - olikkum maṇiyaiyuṭaiya neṭiya tēraik koḷka veṉṟu collik koṭutta; parantu ōṅku ciṟappiṉ pārimakaḷir - parantu mēmpaṭṭa talaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya pārikku makaḷir; yāṉ paricilaṉ-:maṉṉum antaṇaṉ - atuvaṉṟiyum nilaipeṟṟa antaṇaṉ; nī-; varicaiyil vaṇakkum vāḷ mēmpaṭunaṉ - pakaivaraip pōr ceyyu muṟaimaiyāṟ porutu tāḻvikkum vāḷāl mēmpaṭupavaṉ ātalāl; niṉakku yāṉ koṭuppak koṇmati - niṉakku yāṉ tarak koḷvāyāka; aṭaṅkā maṉṉarai yaṭakkum - ciṉattaiyuṭaiya pōrālē vēntarai mikaiyaṭakkum; maṭaṅkā viḷaiyuḷ nāṭu kiḻavōy - maṭakkappaṭāta mikka viḷaitalaiyuṭaiya nāṭṭai yuṭai yōy e-ṟu. 
maṭakkutal - pōkam orukkutal. palaviṉ kaṉi kavarntu uṇṭa kaṭuvaṉ, mantiyoṭu ciṟantu cēṇ viḷaṅkik kaḻai micait tuñcum eṉṟataṉāl, nīyum ivarai varaintukoṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻtal vēṇṭumeṉpatu tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. “yāṉē paricilaṉ” eṉpataṉāl niṉakku eṉ kuṟai muṭikka vēṇṭu meṉpatūum. “antaṇaṉ” eṉṟataṉāl, yāṉ tarutaṟkuriyēṉ eṉpatūum koḷḷappaṭum. 
ulakattu makaṭpēciviṭak koṭuttalai yaṉṟit tāmē ivaraik koḷvāyāka eṉṟu irantu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, itu, pariciṟṟuṟaiyāyiṟṟu. 
viḷakkam : 
malaiyiṭattē niṉṟu kāyppiṉum, palā, kuṟavarāṟ kākkap paṭutaliṉ, kāvala raṟiyātu koṇṭamai tōṉṟa, “palaviṉ kaṉi kavarn tuṇṭa kaṭuvaṉ” eṉṟār. kaṭuvaṉukku viral kaṟut tiruttalum mantikku mukañ civantiruntalum iyalpātaliṉ, “karuviral kaṭuva” ṉeṉṟum “cemmuka manti” yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. malaiyiṭattē palvakaik kāṭcikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvaṉavāyirukka, palākkaṉi yuṇṭa kaṭuvaṉ mantiyoṭu kūṭik kaḻaimicait tuñcum kāṭciyai varain teṭuttōtiyataṉ karuttu itu veṉpārāy, uraikārar “palaviṉ..... tōṟṟi niṉṟatu” eṉṟār. pakaivar uṭalaik kutti niṇattaic citaittaḻippatu paṟṟi, “niṇantiṉṟu cerukkiya” eṉṟār. iḵtu ilakkaṇai. ciṟappu-talaimai. peṇpēcutaṟkum koṭuttaṟkum tūtu pōtalum, kātala riruvaruḷ oruvark koruvaratu kāma  nilai yuraittalum, ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum ceytalum pārppaṉarkku amaiyumeṉat tolkāppiyam mutaliya paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟutaliṉ, “maṉṉum antaṇaṉ” eṉṟār. yāṉ eṉakkuriya muṟaiyil ceyvaṉa ceytalpōla, nīyum niṉakkuriya ceyalākiya vāṇmēm paṭutalai muṟaipiṟaḻātu ceykiṉṟā yeṉpār, “varicaiyil vaṇakkum” eṉṟār. yāṉ antaṇaṉ; nī vāḷ mēmpaṭum marapiṉāy eṉpatām. vāḷ mēmpaṭu marapiṟ ṟōṉṟiyataṟ kēṟpa vēntar mikaiyai yaṭakkukiṉṟāyeṉpār, “aṭaṅkā maṉṉarai yaṭakkum kiḻavōy” eṉṟār. pōkam pala talaiyāp palkip perukutaliṉ, maṭakkutal “pōka morukkutal” eṉap paṭṭatu. orukkutal - varaiyaṟuttal. pārimakaḷiraik kapilar mīḷa iruṅkōvēḷiṭam koṇṭu cellukiṉṟārātaliṉ, viccikkō avar vēṇṭukōḷai ēṟkavillai yeṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

muṟṟilum illāta pāṭalkaḷ: 267'80 268
iṭaiyil illāta pāṭalkaḷ:
282, 283, 285, 288, 306,'80 317, 321, 323, 328, 333, 334, 335, 337, 339, 340, 341, 345, 347, 352, 353, 357, 361, 362, 366, 373, 377, 379, 380, 383, 384, 387, 388, 390, 391, 393, 395, 396, 398, 399, 400.
piṟpakuti illāta pāṭalkaḷ: 244, 355, 361
mutal illāmal irunta pāṭalkaḷ iraṇṭum (328, 370) uraivēntar oḷavaikkuk kiṭaitta cuvaṭikaḷ vaḻiyāka mutal peṟṟaṉa.
iṭaiyil illāta pāṭalkaḷiṉ cila pakutikaḷ nirampiṉa: muḻuvatum nirampiṉavum uṇṭu: avai: 337, 345, 357, 377, 379, 384, 390, 393 eṉpavai.
kuṟippu :
toṭakkam mutal 266, āmpāṭal muṭiyap paḻaiya urai uḷḷatu.
266kku mēl iraṇṭu pāṭalkaḷ (267, 268) kiṭaikkavillai. 282 ām pāṭalukkumēl pāṭalil mutal, iṭai, kaṭai tiṇai, tuṟai, pāṭiyōr, pāṭappaṭṭōr viṭupāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivaṟṟāl uraikaṇṭārālēyē nūlkaḷ kākkappaṭṭaṉa; viḷakkam peṟṟa eṉpaṉa pulappaṭum.
uraivēntar, oḷavai patippāl, patippitta nūlkaḷaiyum ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttaliṉ nalam pulappaṭum. avar patippāl pāṭiyavar pāṭappaṭṭavar peyarkaḷ ceppamum ceḻumaiyum peṟṟaṉa. kalveṭṭu varalāṟu āyvāl avai mēlum teḷivaṭaintaṉa. ivaṟṟai eṇṇal etirkāla nalappāṭām.
